| [.claude](https://github.com/sgl-project/sglang-torchtpu/tree/main/.claude ".claude")  | [.claude](https://github.com/sgl-project/sglang-torchtpu/tree/main/.claude ".claude")  | [Merge remote-tracking branch 'upstream/main' into bot/sync-from-upstr…](https://github.com/sgl-project/sglang-torchtpu/commit/0a509a09b30cc9775d65f48709a089969b271b0e "Merge remote-tracking branch 'upstream/main' into bot/sync-from-upstream-08fa37e48d

Merges upstream sglang 08fa37e48d \(395 commits\) on top of main 2cec1cf194.

Conflict resolution, besides keeping both sides where they only added
lines next to each other:

- layers/communicator -> layer_boundary: upstream retired the layer facade;
  the TPU patches it carried are re-applied in layer_boundary \(plain
  property instead of cached_property on StagePlan.output, UnreducedOutput
  as a slots class and ExitDecision as a NamedTuple so dynamo can build
  them, the rank-uniform residual add under DP attention, and the hybrid
  DP reduce-scatter guard\).
- scheduler.get_internal_state moved into SchedulerInternalStateController;
  the torch.compile readback counters move with it.
- gpt_oss: upstream now captures aux hidden states inside the layer for
  every backend, which supersedes the TPU-only in-layer capture path.
- eagle_worker_common: upstream's fused CUDA chain/commit kernels are gated
  on CUDA tensors and coexist with the TPU device-length / compile-policy
  padding path.
- MoE: the post-experts sum is now owed to the layer boundary, so the TPU
  fused-RS backend reports its EP reduction complete through
  post_experts_output_is_complete instead of the removed model-level call.
- CommonKVSender.clear keeps clear_status\(\) \(raiden overrides it\) and the
  attribute guards the TPU managers rely on.
- .claude/skills is upstream's symlink to .agents/skills; the fork-only
  tpu-nightly-dashboard skill moves there.
- is_chunk_cache / is_tree_cache -> supports_prefix_sharing \(inverted\) and
  cache_unfinished_req -> checkpoint\(req, up_to=...\): the raiden SWA window
  release in disaggregation/prefill.py and the fork's tests follow upstream.
- Dropped: the fork's TPU-only tests for retired code \(layer facade compile
  guard, mla_output_gate _is_tpu patch, internal-state world-size readback\),
  replaced by upstream's equivalents or a new layer_boundary test.

| [docs](https://github.com/sgl-project/sglang-torchtpu/tree/main/docs "docs")  | [docs](https://github.com/sgl-project/sglang-torchtpu/tree/main/docs "docs")  | [[TPU] Kimi-K3 megakernel: fix KDA addressing for DSpark verify, stand…](https://github.com/sgl-project/sglang-torchtpu/commit/c5d58d69e6daa549d781fced8494dcd2030c50c3 "\[TPU\] Kimi-K3 megakernel: fix KDA addressing for DSpark verify, standalone server DSpark \(#1617\)

* \[TPU\] K3 megakernel: address KDA state with the GDN pool's slot + window layout

#1587 changed the TPU mamba pool from `checkpoint_width` rows per slot to one
row per slot plus one `checkpoint_width` verify window per request pool row
\(`window_start + req_row * W`\), with a read offset of AT_SLOT naming the slot
row. The K3 megakernel still addressed rows as `slot * W + read_offset`, so
with speculation \(DSpark\):

- a verify after a prefill read row `slot * W - 1` and wrote its checkpoints
  over other slots' rows, not the window that select_checkpoints, the backend
  and P/D read next;
- extend and decode read and wrote `slot * W` instead of the slot row.

Resolve the rows the way TpuGDNAttnBackend does: a verify reads the committed
row \(committed_state_rows\) and writes the request's window \(window_bases\);
extend and decode read and write the slot row. Without speculation nothing
changes \(both are the slot row\).

* \[TPU\] Kimi-K3 megakernel: verify rows run the MoE down projection on dequantized weights

Speculative verify \(R = 8 rows\), on by default: moe_down_dequant runs an
expert's down projection as one M=R dot on scale-applied bf16 weights
\(exact dequantization: FP4 values times powers of two; f32 reorder of the
K sum\) instead of one dot per scale group.

B1 decode is unchanged.

* \[TPU\] Kimi-K3 megakernel: one-round o_proj gather and far-first copies for verify rows

- oproj_full_gather: the pair o_proj's row-scattered reduce gathers both
  parities' rows in one host round, dropping the halves swap \(same bits\).
  Accepted in serving \(SERVING_KERNEL_OPTIONS\).
- far_first: every collective level issues its copies to other hosts first
  \(issue order only, same bits\); 93L 4 hosts -0.05 ms/verify.

Both on by default; B1 decode is unchanged \(anchor sha 3b3bd4e80add8d64\).

* \[TPU\] Kimi-K3 standalone server: DSpark Markov head as one Pallas kernel

server/draft_kernels.py: the DSpark draft's 7 dependent Markov steps as one
Pallas kernel on the megakernel mesh \(w2 shard resident in VMEM, w1 rows by
DMA, in-kernel exchange\). Used by the JAX draft model in the next commit.

* \[TPU\] Kimi-K3 standalone server: JAX DSpark draft model

server/draft.py: the DSpark block drafter \(5 dense Qwen3-style layers over
the target's captured hidden states, vanilla Markov head\) in plain JAX,
TP-sharded on the megakernel mesh, for the standalone server's on-device
speculative loop. Semantics follow sglang's TPU DSpark path \(greedy,
sample_from_anchor, YaRN RoPE\); the context KV is filled by append_context
from the target's capture_mixture rows, propose drafts 7 tokens for the
block \[anchor, mask x 6\].

- The draft runs on the whole mesh \(default\) or on host-local groups
  \(tp=8: every host its own copy\); its all-reduces and gathers are
  standalone Pallas megakernel collectives \(collective ids 9 and 10\).
- Small-capacity context attention in one masked shot; own transposed
  lm_head copy \(attach_lm_head\).
- R-invariance: propose / append_context are fenced by
  optimization_barrier and RoPE reads a host-built cos|sin table, so the
  drafts do not depend on the enclosing program \(rounds per call\).
- init_state\(sglang_compat=True\) + propose_state reproduce the sglang TPU
  path's extra anchor-row context, for replaying sglang reference dumps.

Single host TP8: propose ~520 us, append_context ~52 us at capacity 8192.

* \[TPU\] Kimi-K3 standalone server: on-device DSpark rounds in the engine

Engine\(draft=...\) adds a speculative path next to the B1 greedy decode:
- prefill and verify kernels also emit the draft's captured layers
  \(capture_mixture; DSpark's \[7, 23, 51, 67, 83\], \[0, 3, 6, 9, 12\] on the
  13-layer stack\); prefill appends every block's captures to the draft;
- generate_spec runs `rounds` rounds per device call: propose 7 drafts,
  one 8-row verify \(KDA checkpoints ping-pong between two 8-row banks\),
  accept the longest prefix equal to the target argmax + bonus, append the
  committed rows' captures to the draft; the host checks every round's
  acceptance from the returned argmax and drops the overshoot;
- the draft weights stay out of the round loop's carry.

13L single host: R=1 == R=8 rounds per call token for token.

* \[TPU\] Kimi-K3 standalone server: serve DSpark \(--spec-draft dspark\)

app.py: --spec-draft dspark --draft-path --draft-tp --spec-rounds; jobs run
generate_spec on a spec engine \(rows prefill\). Benchmark / test hooks
\(k3_force_acc: forced tokens per round, the PR #8 timing protocol;
k3_draft=zero: verify without the draft; k3_stop_ids: extra EOS ids\) are
rejected with 400 unless the server runs with --bench-hooks; the CPU test
covers that.

13L single host: HTTP smoke \(max_tokens / stop / EOS / stream / forced\)
passes.

* \[TPU\] Kimi-K3 standalone server: DSpark manual checks and benchmarks

test/manual/k3_megakernel \(TPU, run by hand; README.md says how to serve
with DSpark and run them\):
- spec_check.py: engine-level check, plain decode vs spec decode at several
  rounds per call with the real draft or a replay / zero stub draft
  \(--verify-mode loop: bit-identical verify, spec == plain decode\);
- spec_smoke.py: HTTP smoke of a running server \(max_tokens, stop strings,
  EOS, stream == non-stream, forced acceptance\);
- spec_bench.py: forced-acceptance timing over HTTP, acceptance / ms per
  round from a server log;
- test_dspark_draft.py: the JAX draft against sglang DSpark reference dumps
  and its latency.

* \[TPU\] Kimi-K3 megakernel: trim the DSpark tests

Keep the CPU checks to the KDA row addressing \(decode / fresh verify /
verify after a commit\) and the --bench-hooks gate; the manual TPU checks
under test/manual/k3_megakernel move to a follow-up.

* \[TPU\] Kimi-K3 megakernel: verify rows run an expert picked by one row on that row only

Speculative verify \(R = 8 rows\), on by default: moe_single_rows runs the
down projection of an expert picked by one row only \(most at R = 8\) on that
row, 12 group rows instead of 12 * R. A zero weight marks an unpicked row \(a
picked row's weight is a normalized sigmoid score, > 0\). Experts picked by
several rows keep the dequantized M=R dot.

13L 1x8: 3.06 -> 2.74 ms/verify. B1 decode is unchanged.

* \[Kimi-K3\]\[TPU\] Cookbook: decode megakernel with DSpark on the standalone server

3.7.6 now shows the megakernel's own server with on-device DSpark \(4 hosts,
93 layers\): 1.42 ms TPOT at 1k in / 8k out \(5.5x the default path\), about
759 tok/s on GSM8K questions at accept length 5.58, full GSM8K 0.978. The
launch command is the measured one. The sglang-runtime megakernel numbers and
commands are dropped from the page.

* \[Kimi-K3\]\[TPU\] Cookbook: label the megakernel's no-DSpark column")  | Oct 21, 2026  |

* \[Refactor\] Nemotron-H: build each layer's boundaries from its stage and the previous one \(#41430\)

* \[Refactor\] Move CuTe DSL-fused layers onto the declared boundaries and FFN-exit kernel entries \(#41431\)

* \[Fix\] Run MoE layers under attention DP and GQA prefill CP on the declared DP × CP gather \(#41432\)

* \[Fix\] Falcon-H1: count the Mamba mixer's output once under tensor parallelism \(#41433\)

* \[Refactor\] Falcon-H1: complete the FFN's sum through ffn_exit \(#41434\)

* \[Refactor\] Choose MoE layers' boundaries from declarations when moe_dp_size equals attn_cp_size \(#41435\)

* \[Fix\] LongCat-Flash under attention DP: branch and merge the dense FFNs through the communicators \(#41436\)

* \[Refactor\] Step-3.5: complete the dense MLP's sum through ffn_exit \(#41437\)

* \[Refactor\] Choose every layer's boundaries from declarations and remove the scatter-mode selection \(#41438\)

* \[Refactor\] Split the layer communicator into a package \(move only\) \(#41439\)

* \[Refactor\] Declare each stage's residual read and update, and give each stage its own entry \(#41440\)

* \[Refactor\] Build the boundary into any stage with one construction \(#41441\)

* \[Refactor\] Give a layer its CuTe DSL kernels at construction instead of a subclass \(#41442\)

* \[Refactor\] Replace LayerScatterModes with LayerFacts and remove ScatterMode \(#41443\)

Co-authored-by: Mick Qian <mickqian@users.noreply.github.com>

* perf\(multimodal\): offload CPU feature hashing with bounded admission \(#39539\)

* \[TPU\] Port the TPU layer-communicator changes into the communicator package

Upstream split layers/communicator.py into a package \(#41439\) and reworked
the reduce-scatter decision. Carry the fork's three TPU changes over:
rank-uniform residual add in the attention-DP partial path, the hybrid
DP-attention reduce-scatter gate, and the enum comparison dynamo can trace.

* \[TPU\] Adapt fork code to upstream's parallel-record and platform changes

- Read pp/dp ranks from get_parallel\(\): upstream retired Scheduler.ps and
  dp_attention.get_attention_dp_rank \(#40343, #40067\).
- TpuPlatform opts out of torch.distributed device_id through the new
  supports_distributed_device_id\(\) hook.
- Tests: the Kimi K3 kernel namespace moved to kernels.ops.attention
  \(#40922\); the PP output recv now returns \(tensors, event\).

* \[Test\] Adapt fork unit tests to upstream's allocator and host-pool changes

- Decode prealloc tests bind the separate-buffer allocator double; the
  queue now asks the allocator to price and reclaim preallocations.
- Host-pool tests pin the budget with host_memory_budget_scope; upstream
  no longer probes psutil in pool_host.base.
- MLP-sync draft-scope tests give the mock runner a NoopCompilePolicy.

* \[TPU\] Move HiRadixCache under hardware_backend/tpu

Upstream removed HiRadixCache \(#40787\), but the torch_tpu HiCache io
backend still builds on it. Move it out of the upstream-shared mem_cache
package and make it self-contained:

- Carry PrefetchTimeoutConfig and a HiTreeNode with the host-side node
  state that upstream dropped from TreeNode, instead of re-adding them to
  shared files.
- Drop the DSA and MiniMax-sparse construction branches; the registry
  routes only non-hybrid, non-DSA models here.
- Provide is_backuped\(\), which upstream removed from BasePrefixCache.

* \[CI\] Run upstream's PR health action test in lint

The fork's CPU stage uses check-pr-test-health; keep its unit test that
upstream added to lint.yml.

* Keep upstream behavior on non-TPU paths the merge changed

Upstream tests caught fork code that diverged from upstream outside TPU:

- disaggregation poll: skip single-rank reductions as upstream now does,
  while keeping the fork's drain gate for multi-rank groups.
- hybrid linear attention: the extend_seq_lens cumsum for query_start_loc
  is a TPU padding workaround; other backends read extend_start_loc again.
- communicator: compare DpPaddingMode directly on TPU only.
- spec_utils imports set_mamba_track_indices_from_reqs at module level as
  upstream does; the import cycle is broken in overlap_utils instead.
- PP mixin reads _pp_wave defensively for callers built without the mixin.
- TPU compiled forward copies the new input_logprobs_copy_done field.

* \[Test\] Adapt TPU unit tests to upstream's runtime-context changes

- Publish or override a consistent topology instead of setting tp_rank or
  Scheduler.ps alone; upstream validates the parallel context.
- PP tests: recv helpers return \(tensors, event\); stages read pp rank and
  size from get_parallel\(\).
- Stub draft_pp_context and the new device_indices_from_anchor_fn field.

* \[TPU\] Read process groups through get_parallel\(\)

Upstream's runtime-context test now rejects callers of the deprecated
get_tp_group/get_attn_tp_group/get_world_group getters outside
sglang.srt.distributed. Move the fork's remaining callers \(TPU sampler,
fused-RS MoE, DFlash, logits global Top-K, EAGLE verify\) and their tests.

* Keep TPU-only checks from reading state other backends never set

- TPU MoE/spec override and forward-hook placement check the cheap,
  always-present condition first, so upstream callers that never publish
  observability or set a device are unaffected.
- The PP decode consensus channel addresses peers through its own
  pp_group, not the process-wide parallel context.
- KV transfer age skips transfer-queue entries without a request.

* \[Test\] Update fork tests for upstream scheduler and configurator changes

- Bare Scheduler/KVCacheConfigurator doubles set the fields upstream added
  \(gracefully_exit, enable_lmcache, attn_dp_size, pp_size\) and drop ps.
- Upstream MLP-sync tests build their runner with NoopCompilePolicy, the
  policy production uses off TPU.
- GptOssModel captures aux hidden states inside the layer in this fork, so
  it is left out of the model-level capture test.

* \[Test\] Point TPU diffusion enablement tests at the new platform hooks

CustomOp dispatch reads platforms.current_platform, and the default
process group asks supports_distributed_device_id\(\); drive both the way
the real TpuPlatform answers.

* \[Test\] Set fork scheduler state in doubles instead of reading it defensively

The repo forbids defensive getattr in production code, so upstream tests
built with bare schedulers/runners now set the fields the fork reads
\(use_overlap_streams, deferred chunked aborts, _pp_wave, runner device,
transfer-queue request stamps\). Also give main's new DFlash precompile
test a consistent parallel topology.

* \[TPU\] Keep GPT-OSS in-layer aux capture on TPU only

Other backends capture boundary hidden states in the model loop as upstream
does, including the reduce_output that completes a deferred FFN sum, so the
upstream aux-capture test covers GptOssModel again; it skips it on TPU,
where capture happens inside the layer.

* \[TPU\] Bump xgrammar to 0.2.7 to match upstream

Upstream function-call parsing now passes parallel_tool_calls to xgrammar's
structural-tag API, which the TPU pin \(0.2.0\) does not accept.

* \[Test\] Fix import order in test_mem_pool_host

* \[Test\] Stub process groups through get_parallel in fused-RS tests

fused_moe_rs_gather and the NVFP4 fused-RS check read get_parallel\(\)
now; the remaining patches of the deprecated distributed getters no
longer reached them \(stage-a-test-tpu-unit failure\).

* \[Test\] Use the 3.10-safe enter_scope in chunked-req-gate tests

unittest.TestCase.enterContext is 3.11+; the CPU CI runs Python 3.10.

* \[CI\] Skip the runner utilization report in this fork

It scans upstream's self-hosted runners; this repository's token gets
HTTP 403, so the job only fails here.

* \[Test\] Pin the host-memory budget in two more host-pool tests

The CPU runners have less free RAM than the 10 GiB HiCache reserve, so
even zero-byte host pools are rejected; book these against a scoped
budget like the other host-pool tests.

* Skip the deprecated-getter caller check while compiling

The wrapper reads sys._getframe to decide whether to warn, which dynamo
cannot trace. TPU compiles whole forwards, and exempt helpers such as
tensor_model_parallel_all_gather call get_tp_group\(\) inside them, so the
GPT-OSS EAGLE draft precompile failed.

* Build FfnCompletion as a NamedTuple

It is constructed inside the forward on every layer. TPU compiles the
whole forward, and dynamo cannot instantiate a msgspec.Struct
\(object.__new__\(FfnCompletion\) is not safe\), which crashed the server at
the first compiled forward.

* Build UnreducedOutput as a plain class

A layer that defers its FFN sum builds one inside the forward; dynamo
cannot instantiate a msgspec.Struct, so a TPU compiled forward on that
path would fail like FfnCompletion did. A NamedTuple would collide with
isinstance\(hidden_states, tuple\) checks, so use a slotted class.

* \[Test\] Use upstream's launch_world_* names in the DFlash TPU worker test

Upstream renamed the parallel context's world_size/world_rank to
launch_world_size/launch_world_rank and retired the per-runner ps record.

* \[Test\] Mirror DFlash's new LiLiCorr and draft-attention fields in the TPU worker double

The fixture bypasses DFlashWorkerV2.__init__; upstream's LiLiCorr reranker
\(#37462\) and draft_owns_attention are read in forward_batch_generation.

* \[Test\] Add the batch and runner fields ForwardBatch.init_new now reads to the DFlash TPU doubles

Found by diffing the batch/model_runner attributes read on the DFlash
verify path against fork main: mamba_*_cpu mirrors,
dp_spec_prefill_coordination_applied, and attn_dcp_*/tp_rank on the
runner \(the per-runner ps record is gone\).

* \[CI\] Skip the NPU nightly image build in this fork

Upstream's npu.Dockerfile change makes this PR trigger it; the arm64
builds fail on a 403 from the Ascend wheel host, and the fork publishes
no NPU images.

* \[Test\] Import the diffusion quantization package before probing compile sites

Importing vocab_parallel_embedding first trips upstream's new cycle
through kitchen_int8_config \(reproducible on pure upstream\); production
imports the quantization package first.

* \[TPU\] Pass attention rank tensors on the direct-compile async warmup

#893 added an async warmup path and #963 made the traced forward take
attention_rank_tensors; only the call path passes them, so every TPU
precompile through warmup_async failed \(GPT-OSS spec server startup\).
Also unpack _direct_compile's \(call, warmup\) pair in #963's test.

* \[TPU\] Free pages directly in TpuHiCacheController.evict_device

Upstream removed HiCacheController.evict_device together with HiRadixCache
\(#40787\); the TPU HiRadixCache still evicts device pages through it, which
failed the hicache_pallas nightly tests with AttributeError.

* \[TPU\] Build LayerCommunicator stage accessors eagerly

Upstream's attn/ffn cached_property first writes the instance __dict__
after warmup traced the layer, so dynamo's guard on the earliest compiled
bucket can never pass again. Under fail_on_recompile the PD prefill server
died on the first request that reused that bucket \(gpt-oss-120b PD
multihost prefix-cache KV-offload nightly\).

* \[TPU\] Make LayerCommunicator stage accessors plain properties

Building attn/ffn in __init__ broke the test doubles that construct a
LayerCommunicator without __init__. A plain property never writes the
instance __dict__, so it keeps dynamo's warmup guards valid the same way.

Co-authored-by: Mick Qian <mickqian@users.noreply.github.com>

* perf\(multimodal\): offload CPU feature hashing with bounded admission \(#39539\)

* \[TPU\] Port the TPU layer-communicator changes into the communicator package

Upstream split layers/communicator.py into a package \(#41439\) and reworked
the reduce-scatter decision. Carry the fork's three TPU changes over:
rank-uniform residual add in the attention-DP partial path, the hybrid
DP-attention reduce-scatter gate, and the enum comparison dynamo can trace.

* \[TPU\] Adapt fork code to upstream's parallel-record and platform changes

- Read pp/dp ranks from get_parallel\(\): upstream retired Scheduler.ps and
  dp_attention.get_attention_dp_rank \(#40343, #40067\).
- TpuPlatform opts out of torch.distributed device_id through the new
  supports_distributed_device_id\(\) hook.
- Tests: the Kimi K3 kernel namespace moved to kernels.ops.attention
  \(#40922\); the PP output recv now returns \(tensors, event\).

* \[Test\] Adapt fork unit tests to upstream's allocator and host-pool changes

- Decode prealloc tests bind the separate-buffer allocator double; the
  queue now asks the allocator to price and reclaim preallocations.
- Host-pool tests pin the budget with host_memory_budget_scope; upstream
  no longer probes psutil in pool_host.base.
- MLP-sync draft-scope tests give the mock runner a NoopCompilePolicy.

* \[TPU\] Move HiRadixCache under hardware_backend/tpu

Upstream removed HiRadixCache \(#40787\), but the torch_tpu HiCache io
backend still builds on it. Move it out of the upstream-shared mem_cache
package and make it self-contained:

- Carry PrefetchTimeoutConfig and a HiTreeNode with the host-side node
  state that upstream dropped from TreeNode, instead of re-adding them to
  shared files.
- Drop the DSA and MiniMax-sparse construction branches; the registry
  routes only non-hybrid, non-DSA models here.
- Provide is_backuped\(\), which upstream removed from BasePrefixCache.

* \[CI\] Run upstream's PR health action test in lint

The fork's CPU stage uses check-pr-test-health; keep its unit test that
upstream added to lint.yml.

* Keep upstream behavior on non-TPU paths the merge changed

Upstream tests caught fork code that diverged from upstream outside TPU:

- disaggregation poll: skip single-rank reductions as upstream now does,
  while keeping the fork's drain gate for multi-rank groups.
- hybrid linear attention: the extend_seq_lens cumsum for query_start_loc
  is a TPU padding workaround; other backends read extend_start_loc again.
- communicator: compare DpPaddingMode directly on TPU only.
- spec_utils imports set_mamba_track_indices_from_reqs at module level as
  upstream does; the import cycle is broken in overlap_utils instead.
- PP mixin reads _pp_wave defensively for callers built without the mixin.
- TPU compiled forward copies the new input_logprobs_copy_done field.

* \[Test\] Adapt TPU unit tests to upstream's runtime-context changes

- Publish or override a consistent topology instead of setting tp_rank or
  Scheduler.ps alone; upstream validates the parallel context.
- PP tests: recv helpers return \(tensors, event\); stages read pp rank and
  size from get_parallel\(\).
- Stub draft_pp_context and the new device_indices_from_anchor_fn field.

* \[TPU\] Read process groups through get_parallel\(\)

Upstream's runtime-context test now rejects callers of the deprecated
get_tp_group/get_attn_tp_group/get_world_group getters outside
sglang.srt.distributed. Move the fork's remaining callers \(TPU sampler,
fused-RS MoE, DFlash, logits global Top-K, EAGLE verify\) and their tests.

* Keep TPU-only checks from reading state other backends never set

- TPU MoE/spec override and forward-hook placement check the cheap,
  always-present condition first, so upstream callers that never publish
  observability or set a device are unaffected.
- The PP decode consensus channel addresses peers through its own
  pp_group, not the process-wide parallel context.
- KV transfer age skips transfer-queue entries without a request.

* \[Test\] Update fork tests for upstream scheduler and configurator changes

- Bare Scheduler/KVCacheConfigurator doubles set the fields upstream added
  \(gracefully_exit, enable_lmcache, attn_dp_size, pp_size\) and drop ps.
- Upstream MLP-sync tests build their runner with NoopCompilePolicy, the
  policy production uses off TPU.
- GptOssModel captures aux hidden states inside the layer in this fork, so
  it is left out of the model-level capture test.

* \[Test\] Point TPU diffusion enablement tests at the new platform hooks

CustomOp dispatch reads platforms.current_platform, and the default
process group asks supports_distributed_device_id\(\); drive both the way
the real TpuPlatform answers.

* \[Test\] Set fork scheduler state in doubles instead of reading it defensively

The repo forbids defensive getattr in production code, so upstream tests
built with bare schedulers/runners now set the fields the fork reads
\(use_overlap_streams, deferred chunked aborts, _pp_wave, runner device,
transfer-queue request stamps\). Also give main's new DFlash precompile
test a consistent parallel topology.

* \[TPU\] Keep GPT-OSS in-layer aux capture on TPU only

Other backends capture boundary hidden states in the model loop as upstream
does, including the reduce_output that completes a deferred FFN sum, so the
upstream aux-capture test covers GptOssModel again; it skips it on TPU,
where capture happens inside the layer.

* \[TPU\] Bump xgrammar to 0.2.7 to match upstream

Upstream function-call parsing now passes parallel_tool_calls to xgrammar's
structural-tag API, which the TPU pin \(0.2.0\) does not accept.

* \[Test\] Fix import order in test_mem_pool_host

* \[Test\] Stub process groups through get_parallel in fused-RS tests

fused_moe_rs_gather and the NVFP4 fused-RS check read get_parallel\(\)
now; the remaining patches of the deprecated distributed getters no
longer reached them \(stage-a-test-tpu-unit failure\).

* \[Test\] Use the 3.10-safe enter_scope in chunked-req-gate tests

unittest.TestCase.enterContext is 3.11+; the CPU CI runs Python 3.10.

* \[CI\] Skip the runner utilization report in this fork

It scans upstream's self-hosted runners; this repository's token gets
HTTP 403, so the job only fails here.

* \[Test\] Pin the host-memory budget in two more host-pool tests

The CPU runners have less free RAM than the 10 GiB HiCache reserve, so
even zero-byte host pools are rejected; book these against a scoped
budget like the other host-pool tests.

* Skip the deprecated-getter caller check while compiling

The wrapper reads sys._getframe to decide whether to warn, which dynamo
cannot trace. TPU compiles whole forwards, and exempt helpers such as
tensor_model_parallel_all_gather call get_tp_group\(\) inside them, so the
GPT-OSS EAGLE draft precompile failed.

* Build FfnCompletion as a NamedTuple

It is constructed inside the forward on every layer. TPU compiles the
whole forward, and dynamo cannot instantiate a msgspec.Struct
\(object.__new__\(FfnCompletion\) is not safe\), which crashed the server at
the first compiled forward.

* Build UnreducedOutput as a plain class

A layer that defers its FFN sum builds one inside the forward; dynamo
cannot instantiate a msgspec.Struct, so a TPU compiled forward on that
path would fail like FfnCompletion did. A NamedTuple would collide with
isinstance\(hidden_states, tuple\) checks, so use a slotted class.

* \[Test\] Use upstream's launch_world_* names in the DFlash TPU worker test

Upstream renamed the parallel context's world_size/world_rank to
launch_world_size/launch_world_rank and retired the per-runner ps record.

* \[Test\] Mirror DFlash's new LiLiCorr and draft-attention fields in the TPU worker double

The fixture bypasses DFlashWorkerV2.__init__; upstream's LiLiCorr reranker
\(#37462\) and draft_owns_attention are read in forward_batch_generation.

* \[Test\] Add the batch and runner fields ForwardBatch.init_new now reads to the DFlash TPU doubles

Found by diffing the batch/model_runner attributes read on the DFlash
verify path against fork main: mamba_*_cpu mirrors,
dp_spec_prefill_coordination_applied, and attn_dcp_*/tp_rank on the
runner \(the per-runner ps record is gone\).

* \[CI\] Skip the NPU nightly image build in this fork

Upstream's npu.Dockerfile change makes this PR trigger it; the arm64
builds fail on a 403 from the Ascend wheel host, and the fork publishes
no NPU images.

* \[Test\] Import the diffusion quantization package before probing compile sites

Importing vocab_parallel_embedding first trips upstream's new cycle
through kitchen_int8_config \(reproducible on pure upstream\); production
imports the quantization package first.

* \[TPU\] Pass attention rank tensors on the direct-compile async warmup

#893 added an async warmup path and #963 made the traced forward take
attention_rank_tensors; only the call path passes them, so every TPU
precompile through warmup_async failed \(GPT-OSS spec server startup\).
Also unpack _direct_compile's \(call, warmup\) pair in #963's test.

* \[TPU\] Free pages directly in TpuHiCacheController.evict_device

Upstream removed HiCacheController.evict_device together with HiRadixCache
\(#40787\); the TPU HiRadixCache still evicts device pages through it, which
failed the hicache_pallas nightly tests with AttributeError.

* \[TPU\] Build LayerCommunicator stage accessors eagerly

Upstream's attn/ffn cached_property first writes the instance __dict__
after warmup traced the layer, so dynamo's guard on the earliest compiled
bucket can never pass again. Under fail_on_recompile the PD prefill server
died on the first request that reused that bucket \(gpt-oss-120b PD
multihost prefix-cache KV-offload nightly\).

* \[TPU\] Make LayerCommunicator stage accessors plain properties

Building attn/ffn in __init__ broke the test doubles that construct a
LayerCommunicator without __init__. A plain property never writes the
instance __dict__, so it keeps dynamo's warmup guards valid the same way.

Co-authored-by: Jimmy Shong <69131491+Jiminator@users.noreply.github.com>
Co-authored-by: Shangming Cai <csmthu@gmail.com>
Co-authored-by: Claude Fable 5 <noreply@anthropic.com>

* Rust server unify datapath for mm and generate requests \(#39679\)

* feat\(npu\): Support returning indexer top-k results \(#39060\)

* Fix chat template cache key order \(#41517\)

* docs: add prefill context parallelism guide and design draft \(#39354\)

* \[XPU\] Bump sglang-kernel-xpu wheel to v0.3.0 \(#41220\)

Co-authored-by: Pramod Kumar <144990617+pramodkumar-habanalabs@users.noreply.github.com>

* \[Kimi-K3\] Merge fused_qkvg_proj into the loader-seeded packed_modules_mapping \(#41164\)

* \[Router\] Give the cache-aware tree a snapshot surface \(1/13\) \(#40687\)

Co-authored-by: Kangyan Zhou <kangyan.zhou@radixark.ai>
Co-authored-by: Claude Opus 5 \(1M context\) <noreply@anthropic.com>

* Let predicate-registered linear-attention models carry the mamba radix-cache leaves \(#41165\)

* \[diffusion\] optimization: populate cpu weight stores before host registration to speedup layerwise-offload initialization \(#40439\)

Co-authored-by: Mick Qian <mickqian@users.noreply.github.com>

* perf\(multimodal\): offload CPU feature hashing with bounded admission \(#39539\)

* \[TPU\] Port the TPU layer-communicator changes into the communicator package

Upstream split layers/communicator.py into a package \(#41439\) and reworked
the reduce-scatter decision. Carry the fork's three TPU changes over:
rank-uniform residual add in the attention-DP partial path, the hybrid
DP-attention reduce-scatter gate, and the enum comparison dynamo can trace.

* \[TPU\] Adapt fork code to upstream's parallel-record and platform changes

- Read pp/dp ranks from get_parallel\(\): upstream retired Scheduler.ps and
  dp_attention.get_attention_dp_rank \(#40343, #40067\).
- TpuPlatform opts out of torch.distributed device_id through the new
  supports_distributed_device_id\(\) hook.
- Tests: the Kimi K3 kernel namespace moved to kernels.ops.attention
  \(#40922\); the PP output recv now returns \(tensors, event\).

* \[Test\] Adapt fork unit tests to upstream's allocator and host-pool changes

- Decode prealloc tests bind the separate-buffer allocator double; the
  queue now asks the allocator to price and reclaim preallocations.
- Host-pool tests pin the budget with host_memory_budget_scope; upstream
  no longer probes psutil in pool_host.base.
- MLP-sync draft-scope tests give the mock runner a NoopCompilePolicy.

* \[TPU\] Move HiRadixCache under hardware_backend/tpu

Upstream removed HiRadixCache \(#40787\), but the torch_tpu HiCache io
backend still builds on it. Move it out of the upstream-shared mem_cache
package and make it self-contained:

- Carry PrefetchTimeoutConfig and a HiTreeNode with the host-side node
  state that upstream dropped from TreeNode, instead of re-adding them to
  shared files.
- Drop the DSA and MiniMax-sparse construction branches; the registry
  routes only non-hybrid, non-DSA models here.
- Provide is_backuped\(\), which upstream removed from BasePrefixCache.

* \[CI\] Run upstream's PR health action test in lint

The fork's CPU stage uses check-pr-test-health; keep its unit test that
upstream added to lint.yml.

* Keep upstream behavior on non-TPU paths the merge changed

Upstream tests caught fork code that diverged from upstream outside TPU:

- disaggregation poll: skip single-rank reductions as upstream now does,
  while keeping the fork's drain gate for multi-rank groups.
- hybrid linear attention: the extend_seq_lens cumsum for query_start_loc
  is a TPU padding workaround; other backends read extend_start_loc again.
- communicator: compare DpPaddingMode directly on TPU only.
- spec_utils imports set_mamba_track_indices_from_reqs at module level as
  upstream does; the import cycle is broken in overlap_utils instead.
- PP mixin reads _pp_wave defensively for callers built without the mixin.
- TPU compiled forward copies the new input_logprobs_copy_done field.

* \[Test\] Adapt TPU unit tests to upstream's runtime-context changes

- Publish or override a consistent topology instead of setting tp_rank or
  Scheduler.ps alone; upstream validates the parallel context.
- PP tests: recv helpers return \(tensors, event\); stages read pp rank and
  size from get_parallel\(\).
- Stub draft_pp_context and the new device_indices_from_anchor_fn field.

* \[TPU\] Read process groups through get_parallel\(\)

Upstream's runtime-context test now rejects callers of the deprecated
get_tp_group/get_attn_tp_group/get_world_group getters outside
sglang.srt.distributed. Move the fork's remaining callers \(TPU sampler,
fused-RS MoE, DFlash, logits global Top-K, EAGLE verify\) and their tests.

* Keep TPU-only checks from reading state other backends never set

- TPU MoE/spec override and forward-hook placement check the cheap,
  always-present condition first, so upstream callers that never publish
  observability or set a device are unaffected.
- The PP decode consensus channel addresses peers through its own
  pp_group, not the process-wide parallel context.
- KV transfer age skips transfer-queue entries without a request.

* \[Test\] Update fork tests for upstream scheduler and configurator changes

- Bare Scheduler/KVCacheConfigurator doubles set the fields upstream added
  \(gracefully_exit, enable_lmcache, attn_dp_size, pp_size\) and drop ps.
- Upstream MLP-sync tests build their runner with NoopCompilePolicy, the
  policy production uses off TPU.
- GptOssModel captures aux hidden states inside the layer in this fork, so
  it is left out of the model-level capture test.

* \[Test\] Point TPU diffusion enablement tests at the new platform hooks

CustomOp dispatch reads platforms.current_platform, and the default
process group asks supports_distributed_device_id\(\); drive both the way
the real TpuPlatform answers.

* \[Test\] Set fork scheduler state in doubles instead of reading it defensively

The repo forbids defensive getattr in production code, so upstream tests
built with bare schedulers/runners now set the fields the fork reads
\(use_overlap_streams, deferred chunked aborts, _pp_wave, runner device,
transfer-queue request stamps\). Also give main's new DFlash precompile
test a consistent parallel topology.

* \[TPU\] Keep GPT-OSS in-layer aux capture on TPU only

Other backends capture boundary hidden states in the model loop as upstream
does, including the reduce_output that completes a deferred FFN sum, so the
upstream aux-capture test covers GptOssModel again; it skips it on TPU,
where capture happens inside the layer.

* \[TPU\] Bump xgrammar to 0.2.7 to match upstream

Upstream function-call parsing now passes parallel_tool_calls to xgrammar's
structural-tag API, which the TPU pin \(0.2.0\) does not accept.

* \[Test\] Fix import order in test_mem_pool_host

* \[Test\] Stub process groups through get_parallel in fused-RS tests

fused_moe_rs_gather and the NVFP4 fused-RS check read get_parallel\(\)
now; the remaining patches of the deprecated distributed getters no
longer reached them \(stage-a-test-tpu-unit failure\).

* \[Test\] Use the 3.10-safe enter_scope in chunked-req-gate tests

unittest.TestCase.enterContext is 3.11+; the CPU CI runs Python 3.10.

* \[CI\] Skip the runner utilization report in this fork

It scans upstream's self-hosted runners; this repository's token gets
HTTP 403, so the job only fails here.

* \[Test\] Pin the host-memory budget in two more host-pool tests

The CPU runners have less free RAM than the 10 GiB HiCache reserve, so
even zero-byte host pools are rejected; book these against a scoped
budget like the other host-pool tests.

* Skip the deprecated-getter caller check while compiling

The wrapper reads sys._getframe to decide whether to warn, which dynamo
cannot trace. TPU compiles whole forwards, and exempt helpers such as
tensor_model_parallel_all_gather call get_tp_group\(\) inside them, so the
GPT-OSS EAGLE draft precompile failed.

* Build FfnCompletion as a NamedTuple

It is constructed inside the forward on every layer. TPU compiles the
whole forward, and dynamo cannot instantiate a msgspec.Struct
\(object.__new__\(FfnCompletion\) is not safe\), which crashed the server at
the first compiled forward.

* Build UnreducedOutput as a plain class

A layer that defers its FFN sum builds one inside the forward; dynamo
cannot instantiate a msgspec.Struct, so a TPU compiled forward on that
path would fail like FfnCompletion did. A NamedTuple would collide with
isinstance\(hidden_states, tuple\) checks, so use a slotted class.

* \[Test\] Use upstream's launch_world_* names in the DFlash TPU worker test

Upstream renamed the parallel context's world_size/world_rank to
launch_world_size/launch_world_rank and retired the per-runner ps record.

* \[Test\] Mirror DFlash's new LiLiCorr and draft-attention fields in the TPU worker double

The fixture bypasses DFlashWorkerV2.__init__; upstream's LiLiCorr reranker
\(#37462\) and draft_owns_attention are read in forward_batch_generation.

* \[Test\] Add the batch and runner fields ForwardBatch.init_new now reads to the DFlash TPU doubles

Found by diffing the batch/model_runner attributes read on the DFlash
verify path against fork main: mamba_*_cpu mirrors,
dp_spec_prefill_coordination_applied, and attn_dcp_*/tp_rank on the
runner \(the per-runner ps record is gone\).

* \[CI\] Skip the NPU nightly image build in this fork

Upstream's npu.Dockerfile change makes this PR trigger it; the arm64
builds fail on a 403 from the Ascend wheel host, and the fork publishes
no NPU images.

* \[Test\] Import the diffusion quantization package before probing compile sites

Importing vocab_parallel_embedding first trips upstream's new cycle
through kitchen_int8_config \(reproducible on pure upstream\); production
imports the quantization package first.

* \[TPU\] Pass attention rank tensors on the direct-compile async warmup

#893 added an async warmup path and #963 made the traced forward take
attention_rank_tensors; only the call path passes them, so every TPU
precompile through warmup_async failed \(GPT-OSS spec server startup\).
Also unpack _direct_compile's \(call, warmup\) pair in #963's test.

* \[TPU\] Free pages directly in TpuHiCacheController.evict_device

Upstream removed HiCacheController.evict_device together with HiRadixCache
\(#40787\); the TPU HiRadixCache still evicts device pages through it, which
failed the hicache_pallas nightly tests with AttributeError.

* \[TPU\] Build LayerCommunicator stage accessors eagerly

Upstream's attn/ffn cached_property first writes the instance __dict__
after warmup traced the layer, so dynamo's guard on the earliest compiled
bucket can never pass again. Under fail_on_recompile the PD prefill server
died on the first request that reused that bucket \(gpt-oss-120b PD
multihost prefix-cache KV-offload nightly\).

* \[TPU\] Make LayerCommunicator stage accessors plain properties

Building attn/ffn in __init__ broke the test doubles that construct a
LayerCommunicator without __init__. A plain property never writes the
instance __dict__, so it keeps dynamo's warmup guards valid the same way.

---------

| [sgl-model-gateway](https://github.com/sgl-project/sglang-torchtpu/tree/main/sgl-model-gateway "sgl-model-gateway")  | [sgl-model-gateway](https://github.com/sgl-project/sglang-torchtpu/tree/main/sgl-model-gateway "sgl-model-gateway")  |  [[TPU/MiniLB] Add session-affinity routing (](https://github.com/sgl-project/sglang-torchtpu/commit/8c23441cb8a3d579accb14db815cd4a344244fe4 "\[TPU/MiniLB\] Add session-affinity routing \(#1691\)

## Why
- PD tests behind MiniLB often need the router to keep a session on
  the same prefill engine, so follow-up turns hit the prefix cache
  the way they do behind the production router. MiniLB picked
  engines at random, so cache hits depended on luck.

## What
- Add an opt-in `--mini-lb-policy session_affinity` that pins each
  `x-routing-key` to a prefill engine and places by sampled load.
- Before: MiniLB chose prefill and decode uniformly at random with
  no load signal, so a multi-turn session landed on a different
  prefill on each turn.
- After: with the policy on, a key stays on its prefill until that
  engine is overloaded, then spills to a less loaded one. Decode goes
  to the least loaded sampled engine. Engines over a guardrail are
  skipped and the request gets 429 when none passes. Engines that
  fail health checks are excluded. `random` stays the default.")[#1691](https://github.com/sgl-project/sglang-torchtpu/pull/1691)[)](https://github.com/sgl-project/sglang-torchtpu/commit/8c23441cb8a3d579accb14db815cd4a344244fe4 "\[TPU/MiniLB\] Add session-affinity routing \(#1691\)

## Why
- PD tests behind MiniLB often need the router to keep a session on
  the same prefill engine, so follow-up turns hit the prefix cache
  the way they do behind the production router. MiniLB picked
  engines at random, so cache hits depended on luck.

## What
- Add an opt-in `--mini-lb-policy session_affinity` that pins each
  `x-routing-key` to a prefill engine and places by sampled load.
- Before: MiniLB chose prefill and decode uniformly at random with
  no load signal, so a multi-turn session landed on a different
  prefill on each turn.
- After: with the policy on, a key stays on its prefill until that
  engine is overloaded, then spills to a less loaded one. Decode goes
  to the least loaded sampled engine. Engines over a guardrail are
  skipped and the request gets 429 when none passes. Engines that
  fail health checks are excluded. `random` stays the default.")  | Oct 23, 2026  |  
| [sglang_tpu](https://github.com/sgl-project/sglang-torchtpu/tree/main/sglang_tpu "sglang_tpu")  | [sglang_tpu](https://github.com/sgl-project/sglang-torchtpu/tree/main/sglang_tpu "sglang_tpu")  | [[TPU][HiCache] Copy-ahead: targeted synchronous waits, retry short ev…](https://github.com/sgl-project/sglang-torchtpu/commit/0fdfd8e4719f638fa3b0d7e35c135891e3adbdb1 "\[TPU\]\[HiCache\] Copy-ahead: targeted synchronous waits, retry short evictions, bound and share the window \(#1612\)

* \[TPU\]\[HiCache\] Pin only the Full host copy of a write_back copy-ahead victim

The copy-ahead window host-locked every component of each victim in the
window. The SWA host lock walks one sliding window of ancestors, takes
them off the SWA host LRU while held, and re-inserts them as most recent
on release. With a victim pinned on nearly every step, SWA host eviction
lands on host leaves instead of ancestors' SWA, deleting the leaves'
Full KV along with it. Duplicate reclaim checks only the Full host lock,
so the window now locks the Full copy alone and leaves SWA and Mamba
host state unlocked.

* \[TPU\]\[HiCache\] Copy-ahead: targeted synchronous waits, retry short evictions, bound and share the window

A synchronous write_back wait \(victim past the window, internal-state
barrier\) synchronized every queued ack, window copies included, so each
such wait paid for unrelated in-flight copies. It now finishes every
non-window ack and, for the barrier backup, the window copy of the node
itself. Window ownership is dropped at the ack so the node's later
synchronous backup, which reuses the ack id, is never mistaken for a
window copy.

An eviction that came up short while window copies locked its victims
returned short, which can retract a request. It now waits for the window
copies once and walks again with the same request.

The SWA eviction order had a window share only when the Full order left
budget unused, which a large evictable set never does. It now has a
reserved quarter of the window, counted in Full tokens like the Full
order, and copies only victims whose tombstone needs a synchronous
backup. Each order keeps its own copies in flight within its share, and a
victim crossing the window's end is no longer copied.")  | Oct 24, 2026  |

A synchronous write_back wait \(victim past the window, internal-state

An eviction that came up short while window copies locked its victims

copies once and walks again with the same request.

The SWA eviction order had a window share only when the Full order left
budget unused, which a large evictable set never does. It now has a
reserved quarter of the window, counted in Full tokens like the Full

backup. Each order keeps its own copies in flight within its share, and a

| [wheel_override](https://github.com/sgl-project/sglang-torchtpu/tree/main/wheel_override "wheel_override")  | [wheel_override](https://github.com/sgl-project/sglang-torchtpu/tree/main/wheel_override "wheel_override")  | [[TPU/CI] Add Nightly TPU workflow with remote wheel override and auto…](https://github.com/sgl-project/sglang-torchtpu/commit/b5b5b42b2569711461e237e5f22adee43f4e2695 "\[TPU/CI\] Add Nightly TPU workflow with remote wheel override and automated baseline comparison

- Adds .github/workflows/nightly-test-tpu-remote-wheel.yml supporting nightly cron and workflow_dispatch with candidate wheel inputs \(package specifier, URL, or GCS\).
- Implements fast single-layer container mutation via Cloud Build \(docker/cloudbuild.tpu-remote-wheel.yaml and docker/tpu-override-wheel.Dockerfile\) reusing the latest nightly base image from main.
- Preserves PEP 427 wheel filenames during staging via wheel_override/ directory.
- Adds automated baseline resolution \(.github/tpu-ci/resolve_nightly_baseline.py\) and GitHub step summary regression/fix diff comparison \(.github/tpu-ci/compare_against_baseline.py\).")  | Sep 15, 2026  |  
| [.bazelrc](https://github.com/sgl-project/sglang-torchtpu/blob/main/.bazelrc ".bazelrc")  | [.bazelrc](https://github.com/sgl-project/sglang-torchtpu/blob/main/.bazelrc ".bazelrc")  | [Serialize Bazel TPU tests and extend evaluation timeout](https://github.com/sgl-project/sglang-torchtpu/commit/191afca64b9039579729154d3def65a849cdfc18 "Serialize Bazel TPU tests and extend evaluation timeout")  | Sep 10, 2026  |  
| [.bazelversion](https://github.com/sgl-project/sglang-torchtpu/blob/main/.bazelversion ".bazelversion")  | [.bazelversion](https://github.com/sgl-project/sglang-torchtpu/blob/main/.bazelversion ".bazelversion")  | [[TPU] Bazel build support for sglang-torchtpu](https://github.com/sgl-project/sglang-torchtpu/commit/ac1f6328826d66d5414a992b4b6c18745595928c "\[TPU\] Bazel build support for sglang-torchtpu

Adds Bazel 8.6 + bzlmod build support, replacing the earlier scaffolding:

- MODULE.bazel pins rules_python, torch_tpu \(source via git_override\), and
  rules_uv/rules_multitool; the pip lock is generated from
  python/pyproject_tpu.toml with `bazel run //requirements:regenerate_lock`
  \(hermetic uv\).
- Fine-grained BUILD files for python/sglang, generated by
  `bazel run //bazel:gen_sglang_build`: acyclic modules keep per-directory
  targets, import cycles collapse into per-directory cycle_N_files filegroups
  feeding cycle_N py_library targets \(a glob cannot cross a package
  boundary\). python/BUILD.bazel is handwritten and defines the :sglang
  aggregator over the generated ALL_MODULE_DEPS.
- The TPU e2e test runs via its original entrypoint \(no wrapper main, no
  recipe hack\). The recipe gets the Bazel Python closure through a real venv
  whose site-packages carries a .pth of runfiles roots \(no PYTHONPATH\), and
  importlib.metadata works through a checked-in sglang dist-info mirroring
  `pip install -e`.
- py_binary launchers use rules_python's script bootstrap so genrule tools
  \(e.g. XNNPACK codegen via XLA\) run on the hermetic toolchain with no PATH
  requirements.

Co-Authored-By: Claude Code <noreply@anthropic.com>")  | Sep 9, 2026  |

* Fix lint after upstream sync

* Adapt TPU integrations to upstream runtime API changes

PR Test Base exposed eight deterministic failures because TPU pools and
focused tests still implemented interfaces from before the upstream sync.

Initialize the ReplaySSM position field expected by the base Mamba pool,
accept the new req_pool_index parameter in TPU KV CPU-copy entry points,
and remove the stale DFlash branch that referenced the renamed draft-input
builder. Update the affected fixtures to provide newly required model,
scheduler, and runtime-context state, and follow the GPT-OSS streamed-weight
helper rename.

These adaptations preserve the TPU behavior: ReplaySSM remains disabled,
req_pool_index remains intentionally unused for the TPU layouts, and the
tests now exercise the same configuration paths used by production.

* Restore SWA budgeting and remaining post-sync contracts

The next PR Test Base run exposed five remaining sync regressions. Most were
test doubles that no longer matched upstream interfaces, but the SWA failure
revealed a real scheduling regression: the PrefillBudget refactor had dropped
the fork's overlap-window headroom cap for chunked-prefill continuations.

Restore that cap in SWAPrefillBudget.fit_chunk while retaining request-ring
behavior. Treat DeepSeek's format_ue8m0 tensor metadata as optional for normal
FP8 checkpoints, add the new is_cpu capability to the MXINT8 platform double,
isolate graph-runner compile assertions from lazy fused-op registry imports,
and include the explicit additional_groups=None argument in the decode poll
assertion.

Together these changes restore the fork's SWA capacity invariant and align the
remaining focused tests with current upstream contracts.

* Restore additive SWA reservation for capped TPU prefill chunks

The upstream PrefillBudget refactor changed a capped continuation from charging the requested chunk plus its future sliding-window headroom to charging only the larger of those values. On TPU's separate SWA pool, that can admit more work than the pool can safely reserve during overlap scheduling.

Restore the additive reservation when allocation_limit is set. Also update the focused MXINT8 and hybrid-pool fixtures with the hf_config and device fields now required by their production configurators.

* Keep shared SWA pools on packed reservation accounting

The additive chunk reservation restored for TPU's separate SWA pool must not apply to SharedSWAPrefillBudget. Unified pools already account for full-attention and SWA demand together in packed byte capacity, so adding the continuation chunk again would over-reserve and unnecessarily reject work.

Make additive_chunk an explicit policy: enable it for SWAPrefillBudget, disable it for shared pools, and cover the separate-pool estimate in the prefill-adder test.

* Track Raiden status reads through the updated dict.get API

CommonKVManager.update_status now reads room state with status.get\(\) instead of subscription. The pausing test double still intercepted __getitem__, so the waiting thread no longer paused inside the transaction and both serialization tests failed their synchronization assertions.

Override get\(\) in both fixtures so they continue to pause at the actual production lookup and verify failure handling and cleanup under the intended interleaving.

* Fix TPU image build after sgl-eval packaging migration

Upstream moved sgl-eval from a Git-pinned helper script into the test extra as a pinned PyPI dependency, but the TPU image build still hashed, copied, and sourced the deleted helper.

Add the upstream sgl-eval and parser pins to pyproject_tpu.toml, let the existing dev_tpu installation install them, and remove the stale helper references from the Dockerfile and base-image cache key.

* Bound TPU base-image dependency resolution

Installing sgl-eval inside the full dev_tpu dependency solve makes pip backtrack through the RunAI and Google Cloud dependency graphs until the one-hour CI credential expires.

Keep the main TPU dependency solve unchanged, then install the pinned sgl-eval and its required parser version in a separate command.

* Adapt TPU sampler fallback test to Gumbel default

* Preserve TPU MXFP4 Pallas runner bypass after sync

* Keep polling decode handshake waiters for failures

* Keep logits mode checks traceable on TPU

* Follow diffusion serialization helpers to their new module

Upstream moved _dumps and CPU tensor transport from sglang.multimodal_gen.runtime.utils.distributed to sglang.multimodal_gen.runtime.distributed.utils. The TPU platform tests still imported the removed path and failed during collection with ImportError: cannot import name distributed from sglang.multimodal_gen.runtime.utils.

Update both the package-level and CI-registered TPU tests to import the relocated module. The patch-target change for current_platform is handled separately once the new ownership is exercised.

* Normalize TPU page IDs released through SWA paths

Upstream SWA allocation began calling _release_page_ids directly. The TPU allocator normalized IDs only in free and free_segment, so an int64 direct release promoted free_pages from int32 to int64. The next decode allocation failed with RuntimeError: Index put requires source and destination dtypes match, got Int destination and Long source.

Override _release_page_ids to move IDs to CPU int32 before returning them to the host allocator, and add lifecycle coverage for the direct-release path.

* Patch diffusion pickle tests at the relocated platform owner

The relocated distributed.utils module now reads platforms.current_platform instead of exposing a current_platform alias. After the import update, both tests still patched dist_utils.current_platform and failed with AttributeError: module sglang.multimodal_gen.runtime.distributed.utils has no attribute current_platform.

Patch dist_utils.platforms.current_platform so the plain-pickle and CPU-transport cases exercise the actual decision point without weakening either assertion.

* Initialize DFlash DP state in the TPU lifecycle fixture

The TPU lifecycle fixture constructs DFlashWorkerV2 with object.__new__, so fields newly created by __init__ are absent. forward_batch_generation accessed _full_embed_gpu, __getattr__ forwarded that lookup to the target-worker double, and CI failed with AttributeError: SimpleNamespace object has no attribute _full_embed_gpu.

Mirror the production non-DP state with _full_embed_gpu set to None and draft_tp_context set to empty_context. This keeps the real TPU forward path under test while disabling only the DP-attention behavior outside this single-rank fixture.

* Provide the draft TP group to the DFlash TPU fixture

DFlashWorkerV2 now enters draft_tp_context\(self.draft_model_runner.tp_group\) around the draft forward. Although this fixture uses empty_context, Python evaluates tp_group before entering it, and CI failed with AttributeError: SimpleNamespace object has no attribute tp_group.

Add tp_group=None to the single-rank draft runner double. The no-op context intentionally ignores it, while production runners continue to pass their real process group.

* Align the DFlash TPU batch fixture with ForwardBatch

ForwardBatch.init_new now copies engram_history and dp_prefill_cuda_graph_max_prefix_len from ScheduleBatch. The hand-built SimpleNamespace omitted both fields, so DFlash verification failed before launch with AttributeError: SimpleNamespace object has no attribute engram_history.

Add neutral None values for this language-only, non-DP-prefill fixture. A static comparison against DFlashWorkerV2.forward_batch_generation, DFlashVerifyInput.prepare_for_verify, and ForwardBatch.init_new confirms that no direct batch fields remain missing.

* Declare the text-only modality contract in DFlash TPU fixtures

ForwardBatch.init_new now calls _maybe_build_forward_token_modalities, which reads model_config.requires_mm_token_modalities. The target and draft model-config doubles predated that capability flag, and CI failed with AttributeError: SimpleNamespace object has no attribute requires_mm_token_modalities.

Set the flag to False on both configs because this lifecycle test is intentionally language-only. The model-config dependency audit also covers is_matryoshka, hidden_size, and model_is_mrope.

* Use the unified resolver for TPU platform defaults

TPU server startup failed before model initialization with ImportError: cannot import name 'declare_late_resolution' from sglang.srt.arg_groups.overrides. Upstream consolidated pre-publish and late declarations into declare_resolution, but the TPU platform still imported and called the removed helper.\\n\\nRoute page size, KV dtype, backend, graph, dtype, and memory defaults through declare_resolution. This preserves the declaration-stash contract and lets TPU servers complete argument resolution.

* Restore TPU staged-free allocator state after sync

GPT-OSS speculative DPA reached scheduler startup and then failed on every rank with AttributeError: 'TpuPagedTokenToKVPoolAllocator' object has no attribute 'num_staged_pages'. The shared paged allocator now stages sorted frees, but the TPU clear override still initialized the removed release_pages representation and called the old merge signature.\\n\\nInitialize staged_pages, num_staged_pages, and free_page_ids_group in the TPU host allocator, and use the parent merge_and_sort_free contract. Page IDs remain CPU int32 through the existing release normalization.

* Return EAGLE verify inputs from draft construction

GPT-OSS speculative DPA completed model and graph initialization, then every scheduler rank failed with AttributeError: 'NoneType' object has no attribute 'is_verify_input'. During the rebase, the PP topology return block was attached after the unconditional return in _fused_draft_steps instead of after build_eagle_verify_input, leaving EagleDraftWorker.draft without a return value.\\n\\nMove the topology-aware and ordinary returns back to draft\(\) and remove the unreachable copy. This restores both TPU fused-draft verification and the PP topology tuple contract.

* Keep TPU CLI defaults serializable in server info

The GPT-OSS TPU server completed warmup, but GET /server_info returned HTTP 500 because FastAPI received a msgspec member_descriptor and raised TypeError: 'member_descriptor' object is not iterable. The manual TPU CLI registrations used ServerArgs class attributes as defaults; on the slotted msgspec struct those are descriptors, not field values.\\n\\nUse None for the three optional topology/hostname argparse defaults. ServerArgs construction then applies the declared field defaults, and resolved_dict remains JSON-serializable.

* Restore TPU options after config namespace replay

The linear rebase dropped choices that had existed only in the original sync merge resolution. That made valid TPU configurations fail argument parsing for Pallas draft attention, TPU/Raiden transfer, fused host-cache layout, and MXINT8 KV caches, and removed the established --tp alias.\\n\\nRestore those TPU choices and keep manually registered TPU compile/topology fields marked no_cli so the namespace-generated parser does not duplicate their custom argument definitions.

* Keep TPU allocator tests on the staged-free contract

The same rebase that restored the obsolete release_pages implementation also rewrote the TPU allocator tests to assert that removed representation. Align the TPU-specific tests with the shared paged allocator's staged_pages and num_staged_pages contract, including host int32 preservation, merge-on-shortfall, and full free-page accounting.

* Preserve full SWA overlap headroom for continuations

Chunked continuations can overlap the preceding chunk before its retained sliding-window state commits. Reserve the full sliding window plus allocator page slack regardless of the remaining decode length, preventing short-generation requests from over-admitting prefill tokens.

* Bypass TPU distributed top-k for sampling-mask batches

Use the current sampling_mask_batch_indices contract when deciding whether distributed top-k is eligible. Sampling-mask batches require full-vocabulary output, so falling through to candidate-only logits would silently omit the requested mask result.

* Repair PR workflow schema and synced-file formatting

Nest rust_ext_artifact under the reusable workflow inputs so GitHub accepts pr-test.yml, and apply the pinned isort and Ruff formatting to files left out of format after the upstream rebase.

* Skip unavailable SMG GPU runners on TPU fork

* Skip unavailable router GPU runner on TPU fork

* Avoid platform probes in compiled non-CP forwards

Three TPU nightly speculative PD jobs failed during graph capture because is_dsa_enable_prefill_cp called is_musa even when attention context parallelism was disabled. Dynamo then attempted the optional torchada import and rejected the full graph.\\n\\nReturn immediately for attn_cp_size <= 1 before backend detection, preserving the existing result while keeping optional platform imports outside the compiled path. Restore the focused upstream regression test that verifies disabled DSA CP never calls the HIP or MUSA probes.

* Stamp TPU compile warmups with sequence sharding

* Expose diffusion perf fixture to TPU nightly tests

The upstream DiffusionServerBase now has an autouse fixture that requests perf_results, but pytest only discovers its defining conftest for tests collected under the multimodal test directory. The registered TPU diffusion test lives elsewhere and therefore fails during setup before launching a model.

Re-export the fixture from the TPU registered-test root, matching the existing AMD adapter, so all descendant TPU diffusion tests can resolve it without duplicating fixture behavior.

* Keep TPU diffusion fallbacks off CPU-only kernels

The synced CPU scale/shift optimization changed the shared cpu fallback to an sgl_kernel operator. Diffusion platform dispatch still classified TPU as cpu, so Wan2.2 failed during denoising with ModuleNotFoundError: No module named 'sgl_kernel'.

Give TPU a distinct platform key and explicitly retain the portable PyTorch fallbacks for scale/shift, rotary embedding, and normalization. This preserves TPU's pre-sync behavior without changing CUDA, CPU, NPU, MPS, MUSA, or XPU dispatch. Add a regression test that proves TPU no longer inherits the CPU fallback.

* Keep diffusion dispatch test free of optional media deps

The TPU platform dispatch regression test imported current_platform through the multimodal package. CPU CI does not install optional imageio support, so test collection failed before exercising the dispatch logic.

Install a scoped stand-in for the lazily imported platforms module instead. This keeps the test focused on platform selection and avoids changing production behavior or broadening the CPU test dependency set.

* Allow TPU diffusion video jobs to finish

Wan2.2 stayed healthy and generated for 1,399.6 seconds, but the shared server test aborted non-AMD jobs after 1,200 seconds. That made the new TPU nightly coverage fail despite no runtime exception.

Give TPU the existing 2,400-second slow-accelerator deadline while retaining a hard failure if generation still does not complete. Other platform timeouts and inference behavior are unchanged.

* Bound TPU Wan nightly to a smoke-sized render

The initial 1280x704, 25-frame request did not complete even after extending its polling window from 20 to 40 minutes. The server remained healthy, showing that the workload was unsuitable for a functional nightly smoke test rather than exposing another kernel crash.

Use the established 832x480, 9-frame, 4-step video shape and restore the shared timeout policy. The test still executes text encoding, denoising, decoding, and MP4 frame validation while fitting the TPU nightly budget.

* Restore TPU Wan job budget for compile warmup

* Fix TPU input pruning across MLIR dialect versions

Direct-compile pruning parsed raw TorchTPU MLIR bytecode with JAX, which can reject newer builtin attribute encodings before server startup.

Serialize the module to a versioned StableHLO portable artifact and deserialize it through JAX's StableHLO API before inspecting operations. This preserves input pruning while replacing the temporary fallback that disabled pruning after parse failures; the tests cover the incompatible raw artifact and the portable round trip.

* Preserve TPU sampler mask-capture contract after sync

Upstream sampling-mask support changed Sampler._sample_from_probs to return both sampled token IDs and optional capture state. The TPU override still returned only the token tensor, so ordinary stochastic requests crashed when the base forward path unpacked the result.

Return the shared tuple contract from every TPU sampling path and capture the filtered support for opted-in sampling-mask rows, including native global top-k token IDs. Extend the TPU sampler unit coverage for the simple path, mask capture, and existing fallback paths.

* Restore Ruff formatting after current-main rebase

Recreating the current-main merge restored two pre-Ruff parenthesized-with forms from the merge parents. Ruff 0.15.1 reformats only those context-manager statements; the 18 Pallas attention tests and 55 host-query-length tests pass unchanged.
